- Returning to the anti-magic barrier, Edvaldo asked to be untied; Zalthir answered, “No," and Gyrgum offered to consider freeing him for a useful answer [ch 069 / 069.06].
- The party reviewed the instructions—the phrase must be spoken by Gyrgum in the dark, as a gift not a demand, only once—and returned upstairs no more certain of the answer, considering candles, wax, and darkness itself [ch 069 / 069.06; ch 069 / 069.07].
- Back in the dark upper chamber with Dawnbringer the sole light, the party debated the answer; Daz observed, “I mean, darkness gets consumed when you light," and the group remained wary of wasting their one permitted utterance [ch 069 / 069.07].
- Gyrgum suggested postponing the attempt to puzzle over the conundrum; the party considered asking Manshoon how the phrase should be spoken, consulting the owlbear, or returning to the dragon for guidance [ch 069 / 069.07].
- The session ended with the riddle unsolved and the Obsidian Door still sealed [ch 069 / 069.07].
- Daz recovered a legendary black Robe of the Archmagi from the defeated simulacrum; he could wear it immediately for style, but its powers required attunement at the next long rest and might require replacing an existing attuned item [ch 070 / 070.01; ch 070 / items].
- Manshoon, the second simulacrum, explained that he had spent staggering amounts of magic and a Staff of Power breaching Candlekeep after the party disrupted his plans involving Alkrist, reducing him from arriving at full strength to bargaining [ch 070 / 070.01; ch 070 / npcs].
- Manshoon relayed Alaundo's inscription on the final door: “To the last door, I gave no lock, for a lock is a promise that a thing can be stolen. I gave it a question instead,” with the question “What is consumed to make light? Say the answer in the dark” [ch 070 / 070.01].
- Manshoon explained the door could only be opened by a verified Reader speaking the correct answer once, in darkness, freely and with genuine intent, and passed the party a note with the answer: a candle [ch 070 / 070.01].
- The party debated whether to trust a former leader of the Zhentarim who once made a bargain with Bane and attempted to conquer the world [ch 070 / 070.01].
- Zalthir interrupted, saying “Before you say, before you say it, I want to punch him,” struck Manshoon, and seized the simulacrum in a grapple [ch 070 / 070.02; ch 070 / moment].
- The individual the party had left tied up appeared at the top of the stairs, revealed as Edvaldo, a pale, featureless doppelganger who had escaped his ropes by changing shape [ch 070 / 070.02; ch 070 / npcs].
- Edvaldo warned that the real Manshoon had installed a fail-safe in his simulacrum: if it died, it would explode, triggering glyphs of warding in a catastrophic chain reaction [ch 070 / 070.02; ch 070 / spells].
- The simulacrum warned that killing him would earn the real Manshoon's vengeance forever, but Zalthir was unmoved, observing “We already killed one of you. I figure it's a good given at this point” [ch 070 / 070.02].
- Zalthir used his extraordinary monk speed to drag the screaming simulacrum out of the room, down the stairs, past Edvaldo and the dead owlbear's bones, across the bridge, and into the dragon's anti-magic field [ch 070 / 070.02; ch 070 / locations].
- Zalthir considered that killing the simulacrum might trigger a dead-man switch destroying the prophecy crystals, but accepted the risk: “Yeah, no, I let him go. I toss him over the edge,” throwing Manshoon into the lava [ch 070 / 070.02; ch 070 / moment].
- Edvaldo, panicked, smashed two of the prophecy crystals with a piece of the dead owlbear's remains, threatening to destroy the rest unless the party opened the door [ch 070 / 070.03; ch 070 / npcs].
- Daz pointed out that smashing the crystals was not persuasive: “Smashing the crystals isn't going to help. That's not going to motivate us to open the door” [ch 070 / 070.03].
- Gyrgum, tired of the standoff, spoke the answer aloud with genuine intent: “A candle,” and the ancient door swung open [ch 070 / 070.03; ch 070 / moment].
- Beyond the door lay a dark room with a single book resting on a podium [ch 070 / 070.03; ch 070 / locations].
- Daz used Misty Step to teleport beside the podium, then created a Minor Illusion of his hand descending, the book sparkling, and collapsing into ash, concealing the real book; Edvaldo believed the illusion [ch 070 / 070.03; ch 070 / spells].
- Thorin stepped between Edvaldo and the remaining gems and used a Menacing Attack with Dawnbringer to frighten the doppelganger [ch 070 / 070.03; ch 070 / items].
- Zalthir returned, punched and grappled Edvaldo, and dragged him toward the stairs; Edvaldo changed into a halfling to escape and fled along the only route not blocked by Thorin, toward the anti-magic barrier [ch 070 / 070.03; ch 070 / npcs].
- Gyrgum seized the four surviving prophecy crystals, declaring enough people had threatened the party with them, and prepared a Guiding Bolt to strike Edvaldo if he moved toward the gems [ch 070 / 070.03; ch 070 / spells].
- Daz identified the book as the Book of Vile Darkness, a sentient artifact of cosmic evil written or purportedly written by the Archlich Vecna, which could not be permanently destroyed while evil existed [ch 070 / 070.04; ch 070 / items].
- Daz, unwilling to touch the book directly, used Mage Hand to flip its pages and resisted its corrupting influence through willpower, avoiding potentially dire consequences [ch 070 / 070.04; ch 070 / spells].
- The sentient book spoke, offering Daz a choice: “Do you want to know something about yourself or do you want to know something about how to defeat the demons?” [ch 070 / 070.04; ch 070 / moment].
- Daz chose knowledge of the demons, believing self-knowledge was something a person had to earn independently; the book began revealing information about events in the Underdark from a narrator he could not trust [ch 070 / 070.04; ch 070 / moment].
- The session ended with the surviving Avowed Readers expected to arrive within minutes, unlikely to permit the party to carry away artifacts of extraordinary power unnoticed [ch 070 / 070.04; ch 070 / locations].
